Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fossils theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 807 and the invariant I(A,B) = 373 mod 3.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 807 + O(1/n^373). Summing over all modes and applying the fossils convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
